## Coverage limits

Covers routing profiles, sorted/grouped row contracts, two-stage expert compute, shared partials and communication lifetimes.
Historical GEMM tile/MFMA precedents remain in gemm_decisions.md; their older source pin is retained.
TP device-communication precedents do not establish arbitrary EP topology support.
No CK expert-template expansion, tuned MoE database extraction or multi-GPU validation is claimed.
Native OPUS source is classified as HIP/C++; binary internals remain uninspected.

These are pinned test sources, not test results. No GPU test was run to produce this corpus.
